Your pool doesn't have error handlers! See: https://err.red/wpeh?v=0.18.0
▶ notifications on PostgreSQL 17 (BE-06, worker side)
  ▶ the send job
    ✔ e-mail: renders the published version in the person's language, attaches the .ics, stores the vendor reference and no address or text (47.379368ms)
    ✔ language: the person's language when published, else the default; SMS and push render without a subject/with a title (30.924461ms)
    ✔ TC-NT-08: SMS off and e-mail on - the SMS is suppressed with its reason and the provider is never called; e-mail is sent (18.146943ms)
    ✔ suppression reasons: no address, unknown recipient, inactive patient, no contributor, no template (43.126036ms)
    ✔ in-app notices need no vendor and are sent even when every external channel is off (TC-NT-04) (6.37392ms)
    ✔ TC-NT-04: a failing provider is retried by the queue; the failure is an error CLASS; the last attempt publishes delivery_failed; recovery sends with the same token (37.002635ms)
    ✔ a vendor refusal (4xx) is final: failed, no queue retry, delivery_failed published (13.23199ms)
    ✔ quiet hours defer the send to when they end, restore the attempt count and queue the job (18.767577ms)
    ✔ a template that leaks text is stopped at send time by the output scan; the provider is never called (8.729751ms)
    ✔ a render error (a declared value missing) is a final failure, not a retry (11.604456ms)
    ✔ BE06-F1: a merge that re-points the row during the vendor call - sent, failed and deferred outcomes are still recorded (under the survivor) (40.083897ms)
    ✔ a patient merged while the job waited: the job follows the survivor and sends to the survivor's contacts (on_merged = resolve) (13.127332ms)
    ✔ a staff notice runs under the organisation scope and resolves through the principal contributor (9.887573ms)
    ✔ no address, message text or canary reaches a log line (0.17013ms)
  ✔ the send job (300.271772ms)
  ▶ delivery receipts (33.1 notifications.on_receipt, SBR-16)
    ✔ a receipt for a kept notification of a merged patient attaches to the SURVIVOR; a repeat of the event is a no-op (17.501952ms)
    ✔ untrusted payloads: unknown reference, bad shape, bad status, oversized and ambiguous references record nothing (10.628955ms)
    ✔ the inbound handler records through the same path (2.788963ms)
    ✔ the worker role cannot update or delete a receipt, or write one for a patient outside its job scope (4.175425ms)
  ✔ delivery receipts (33.1 notifications.on_receipt, SBR-16) (37.395131ms)
  ▶ channel adapters implement the BE-02 interface
    ✔ argument checks reject header injection, malformed addresses and oversize text without echoing them (0.834005ms)
    ✔ provider errors map to AdapterFailure with the provider's retryability; any other exception is retryable; health never throws (0.451498ms)
  ✔ channel adapters implement the BE-02 interface (1.407542ms)
✔ notifications on PostgreSQL 17 (BE-06, worker side) (1729.519273ms)
